Teaching is interdisciplinary and based on the modular-integrative concept. We combine related subjects into networked teaching units (modules), linking methods and theory with specialist applications (integration). In this way, you can quickly recognise the sense and benefit of methods and theories and will acquire knowledge more easily and sustainably. Academic Writing, Effective Communication, and Personal Mastery Bachelor's thesis Business studies with a degree from EBS Universität – you spend five semesters at EBS Universität and one semester abroad. Optionally, the programme can be extended by a seventh semester to increase the ECTS to be earned to 210. You will spend the first part of your studies at EBS Universität and then transfer to one of our partner universities for the second part of your studies. This will give you a Bachelor of Science degree from EBS and a degree from your university abroad. A distinctive feature of the Bachelor in Business Studies at EBS is that you can study according to your interests, goals and talent already in the Bachelor's programme. You decide on a specific focus in the fifth semester. Projects with partners in Germany and abroad Students who choose the international dual degree option spend three semesters at a partner university abroad. In addition to the EBS Bachelor's degree, these students earn the corresponding degree from their partner university. We focus on the quality of teaching. For the Bachelor's in Business study programme at EBS, you need to plan for the following investment . Academic admission requirements Language requirements Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Nestled in the picturesque town of Oestrich-Winkel near the financial hub of Frankfurt, EBS offers a unique academic experience beyond education; it's your gateway to a world of opportunity and personal growth. With an international focus and a strong network, our Master's programmes are designed to elevate your skills and career prospects."

Combined Master's degree / PhD programme Research Forum: Developing PhD Research Projects Projects with partners in Germany and abroad Academic admission requirements Applicants must submit a (maximum three-page) proposal for the dissertation project in English that demonstrates relevance and fit to the research topics of EBS Business School. The final admission decision is made by a committee that meets twice a year and consists of the members of the PhD Committee, the Dean of the Business School and the Vice Dean of Research. The programme can be shortened to two semesters (accelerated track | 60 ECTS) with the appropriate qualifications. With an additional semester abroad (dual degree option), you have the opportunity to acquire a double degree. Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Combined Master's degree / PhD programme With the Master in Management programme, all doors are open to you. Graduates take on leadership positions in a wide variety of areas: as a management consultant, business developer, successor in a family business, founder or business partner. The programme comprises four semesters and concludes with a Master’s thesis – often in cooperation with a company. The last semester is reserved for your stay abroad at one of our more than 200 partner universities. Every person is different: several ways to do a Master’s degree at EBS Universität We have designed the EBS Master's programmes to be flexible, depending on your level of knowledge, skills and goals. Take the study path that best suits you and your circumstances If applicants meet the relevant requirements (Bachelor's degree with 240 ECTS) and have already gained experience abroad, they can shorten the Master's programme to two semesters. Projects with partners in Germany and abroad Semester Abroad Track (120 ECTS) Practice Track (120 ECTS) Academic admission requirements A Bachelor’s degree and transcripts in any field of study with at least 180 ECTS or an equivalent recognised qualification A valid GMAT (either classic or new Focus edition – scores will be based on GMAC's percentile tables), GRE, Business Admissions Test (BAT), EBSgrad Test, Common Admissions Test (CAT). If your application documents meet our admission requirements, we will invite you to an individual interview. During this interview, we will assess your motivation and social skills. The decisive factors are your motivation to perform and learn, your social communication profile, your willingness to take on social responsibility and your initiative. Language requirements TOEFL iBT score min. 95 IELTS min. band score 7 Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Nestled in the picturesque town of Oestrich-Winkel near the financial hub of Frankfurt, EBS offers a unique academic experience beyond education; it's your gateway to a world of opportunity and personal growth.                   "text": "Master's in Management (MSc) Master's in Management Master of Science in Management The programme comprises four semesters (120 ECTS) and can be started each year in the autumn semester or spring semester. The programme can be shortened to two semesters (accelerated track | 60 ECTS) with the appropriate qualifications. With an additional semester abroad (dual degree option), you have the opportunity to acquire a double degree. Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Combined Master's degree / PhD programme With the Master in Management programme, all doors are open to you. Graduates take on leadership positions in a wide variety of areas: as a management consultant, business developer, successor in a family business, founder or business partner. The programme comprises four semesters and concludes with a Master’s thesis – often in cooperation with a company. The last semester is reserved for your stay abroad at one of our more than 200 partner universities. Every person is different: several ways to do a Master’s degree at EBS Universität We have designed the EBS Master's programmes to be flexible, depending on your level of knowledge, skills and goals. Take the study path that best suits you and your circumstances If applicants meet the relevant requirements (Bachelor's degree with 240 ECTS) and have already gained experience abroad, they can shorten the Master's programme to two semesters. Projects with partners in Germany and abroad Semester Abroad Track (120 ECTS) Practice Track (120 ECTS) Academic admission requirements A Bachelor’s degree and transcripts in any field of study with at least 180 ECTS or an equivalent recognised qualification A valid GMAT (either classic or new Focus edition – scores will be based on GMAC's percentile tables), GRE, Business Admissions Test (BAT), EBSgrad Test, Common Admissions Test (CAT). If your application documents meet our admission requirements, we will invite you to an individual interview. During this interview, we will assess your motivation and social skills. The decisive factors are your motivation to perform and learn, your social communication profile, your willingness to take on social responsibility and your initiative. Language requirements TOEFL iBT score min. 95 IELTS min. band score 7 Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Nestled in the picturesque town of Oestrich-Winkel near the financial hub of Frankfurt, EBS offers a unique academic experience beyond education; it's your gateway to a world of opportunity and personal growth. With an international focus and a strong network, our Master's programmes are designed to elevate your skills and career prospects."

A distinctive feature of the Bachelor in Business Studies at EBS is that you can study according to your interests, goals and talent already in the Bachelor's programme.                   "text": "Master's in Business Analytics (MSc) Master's in Business Analytics Master of Science in Business Analytics The programme comprises four semesters (120 ECTS) and can be started each year in the autumn or spring semesters. With the appropriate qualifications, the programme can be shortened to two semesters (accelerated track | 60 ECTS). With an additional semester abroad (dual degree option), you have the opportunity to acquire a double degree. Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Combined Master's degree / PhD programme The Master's in Business Analytics programme is divided into different stages. At the beginning of the programme, we will prepare you for your future research work and support you in the further development of your leadership skills. Building on this, you will deal with the essential issues of your field, consolidate newly acquired knowledge and grow as a leader. We have designed the EBS Master's programmes to be flexible, depending on your level of knowledge, skills and goals. Accelerate your career. If you meet the relevant requirements and have already gained experience abroad, you can shorten the Master's programme to two semesters. You can obtain another international degree (MBA or Master's) in addition to your EBS degree. Extend your study abroad at one of our partner universities by one semester and receive a double degree. In this way, you will gain more expertise in a broader international context, and you will increase your global market value. Projects with partners in Germany and abroad Semester Abroad Track (120 ECTS) Practice Track (120 ECTS) Academic admission requirements A Bachelor’s degree and transcripts in any field of study with at least 180 ECTS or an equivalent recognised qualification. A valid GMAT (either classic or new Focus edition – scores will be based on GMAC's percentile tables), GRE, Business Admissions Test (BAT), EBSgrad Test, Common Admissions Test (CAT) If your application documents meet our admission requirements, we will invite you to an individual interview. During this interview, we will assess your motivation and social skills. The decisive factors are your motivation to perform and learn, your social communication profile, your willingness to take on social responsibility, and your initiative. Language requirements TOEFL iBT score min. 95 IELTS min. band score 7 Application recommendation for non-EU applicants with visa requirements: To start in the autumn semester: end of May To start in the spring semester: end of September Nestled in the picturesque town of Oestrich-Winkel near the financial hub of Frankfurt, EBS offers a unique academic experience beyond education; it's your gateway to a world of opportunity and personal growth. With an international focus and a strong network, our Master's programmes are designed to elevate your skills and career prospects."

                  "text": "A bachelor‘s degree and transcripts in any field of study with at least 180 ECTS or equivalent recognised qualification from an equally recognised institution. Proof of at least 2 years of postgraduate full-time working experience. English language certificate (TOEFL® iBT/MyBest TOEFL: minimum 95; IELTS: 7; Duolingo English Test: minimum 125 or an equivalent certificate). Upon proof, if you have completed an undergraduate degree in English, are a native speaker, or have worked for at least 2 years in an English-speaking country, no certificate is required. All candidates interested in studying abroad as part of the programme need to present a valid TOEFL® or IELTS score due to regulations pertaining to our partner universities. A valid GMAT (either classic or new Focus edition - scores will be based on GMAC's percentile tables), GRE, Business Admissions Test (BAT) , EBSgrad Test, Common Admissions Test (CAT). It is also an industry with a particular need for values and a responsibility towards people. At EBS Universität, we offer you state-of-the-art teaching content, individual personal development and practical work with the big names from the finance industry. So you are perfectly prepared for an exciting future. Welcome to the EBS family. The most important part of your studies is not only 'what', but especially 'with whom'. The EBS family is an exceptional community. International, inspiring, innovative. You are not studying with strangers but with friends, with like-minded people.
